A key capability exhibited in CoT reasoning is self-reflection: revisiting earlier decisions and correcting previous errors. However, existing LVLMs often fail to properly attend to visual inputs during reflection, limiting their ability to translate feedback into grounded corrections, especially for out-of-distribution images. To address this issue, we propose a novel reinforcement learning training framework VRRL, with two components explicitly designed to elicit visually grounded self-reflection. First, we randomly mask trajectory prefixes during training to emphasize recovery from incorrect intermediate predictions rather than making early mistakes. Second, we introduce buffered roll-ins from an experience replay buffer to expose the model to diverse failure states that it must learn to correct. We evaluate our approach on visual grounding tasks involving tables and charts, as well as spatial navigation benchmarks. While off-the-shelf and conventionally fine-tuned models degrade substantially under distribution shift, our method substantially improves average out-of-distribution accuracy over standard RL and reflection-oriented fine-tuning baselines by using self-reflection effectively.

</details>


### [146] [Reasoning LLM Improves Speaker Recognition in Long-form TV Dramas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02504)
*Yuxuan Li,Lingxi Xie,Xinyue Huo,Jihao Qiu,Jiacheng Shao,Pengfei Chen,Jiannan Ge,Kaiwen Duan,Qi Tian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对长篇电视剧中的说话人识别问题，构建了包含53.2万条标注对话的DramaSR-532K基准数据集，并提出了基于大型推理模型的DramaSR-LRM方法，通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据，在短语句识别上显著超越现有基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长篇电视剧的复杂叙事理解中，说话人识别（将每句台词准确归因到对应角色）是关键挑战，现有方法在短语句（声学生物特征不可靠）上表现不佳，亟需融合听觉、语言和视觉线索的鲁棒方法。

Method: (1) 构建DramaSR-532K大规模基准，包含532K条标注对话行、900+独特角色，支持多模态（听觉、语言、视觉）线索融合；(2) 提出DramaSR-LRM，基于大型推理模型，通过多模态工具使用自主聚合上下文证据实现高保真归因。

Result: DramaSR-LRM显著优于现有基线，尤其在短语句上表现突出（因声学生物特征在此场景下不可靠）。

Conclusion: 数据与代码将开源发布，为长篇视频理解中的说话人识别任务提供新基准与有效方法。

Abstract: Long-form TV dramas present a formidable challenge for comprehensive video understanding, where deciphering complex storyline often relies on \textbf{speaker recognition}, the task of accurately attributing each spoken utterance to its respective character. In this paper, we advance this field through two primary contributions. (1) We introduce \textbf{DramaSR-532K}, a large-scale benchmark comprising 532K annotated dialogue lines across more than 900 unique characters, necessitating the integration of auditory, linguistic, and visual cues for speaker recognition. (2) We propose \textbf{DramaSR-LRM}, a robust approach built upon a large reasoning model (LRM). DramaSR-LRM is designed to autonomously aggregate contextual evidence via multimodal tool-use, synthesizing diverse inputs to achieve high-fidelity attribution. Experimental results demonstrate that DramaSR-LRM significantly outperforms existing baselines, particularly on short utterances where acoustic biometrics are inherently unreliable. \textit{All the data and code will be made publicly available at the project page: https://www.github.com/198808xc/DramaSR-LRM.}

</details>


### [147] [LACUNA: A Testbed for Evaluating Localization Precision for LLM Unlearning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02513)
*Matteo Boglioni,Thibault Rousset,Siva Reddy,Marius Mosbach,Verna Dankers*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出LACUNA测试平台，通过将合成PII注入预定义参数来评估机器遗忘方法是否真正擦除知识而非掩盖知识。实验表明现有SOTA遗忘方法虽然输出级表现良好，但定位不精确且易遭受重现攻击；而精确定位后，简单梯度方法即可实现可靠擦除。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遗忘基准仅在输出层面评估效果，无法判断模型是否真正从参数中擦除了知识还是仅仅掩盖了知识，重现攻击的成功进一步凸显了这一问题的严重性。

Method: 通过掩码连续预训练将合成个人的PII注入到1B和7B OLMo模型的预定义参数中，构建具有参数级真实定位的测试平台LACUNA，从而直接评估遗忘方法是否针对负责知识存储的权重。

Result: 现有SOTA遗忘方法在输出级表现良好，但参数级定位高度不精确，且易受重现攻击；当定位成功时，即使简单的梯度遗忘方法也能实现强擦除和对重现攻击的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 精确定位的遗忘至关重要，LACUNA可补充行为评估，推动基于定位的鲁棒遗忘方法研究。

Abstract: LLMs memorize sensitive training data, including personally identifiable information (PII), creating a pressing need for reliable post hoc removal methods. Unlearning has emerged as a promising solution, with state-of-the-art(SOTA) methods often following a localize-first, unlearn-second paradigm that targets specific model parameters. However, existing benchmarks evaluate unlearning solely at the output level, leaving open the question of whether unlearning truly erases knowledge from a model's parameters or merely obfuscates it, a concern reinforced by the success of resurfacing attacks. To bridge this gap, we introduce LACUNA: the first unlearning testbed with ground-truth parameter-level localization. LACUNA injects PII of synthetic individuals into predefined parameters of 1B and 7B OLMo-based models via masked continual pretraining, enabling direct evaluation of whether unlearning targets the weights responsible for knowledge storage. We use LACUNA to benchmark current SOTA unlearning methods and find that, despite strong output-level performance, existing methods are highly imprecise and susceptible to resurfacing attacks. We further show that when localization is successful, even a simple gradient-based unlearning method achieves strong erasure and robustness to resurfacing attacks, highlighting the importance of precise unlearning. We release LACUNA to complement behavioral evaluations and drive further advances in robust, localization-based unlearning.

</details>


<div id='cs.HC'></div>

# cs.HC [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [ExPerT: Personalizing LLM Responses to Users' Domain Expertise via Query-Wise Semantic and Keystroke Behavioral Cues](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01242)
*Yeji Park,Jiwon Tark,Taesik Gong*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: ExPerT是一个查询级个性化框架，通过结合语义（查询文本）和行为（击键动态）线索推断用户领域专业知识，并据此调整LLM响应的详细程度、术语和概念复杂度，实验表明专业知识推断误差降低65.7%，响应满意度提升17.52%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM个性化方法依赖静态用户画像或纯文本信号，无法捕捉用户在不同查询上的专业知识差异，导致响应与用户实际水平不匹配。

Method: 提出ExPerT框架，包含两个核心组件：(i) 语义-行为专业知识推断模块，通过上下文LLM提示联合解释查询文本和击键动态；(ii) 专业知识条件响应生成，根据推断的专业水平调整回答的详细程度、术语使用和概念复杂度。

Result: 在40名参与者、1270个查询的用户研究中，ExPerT相比最强基线将专业知识推断误差降低了65.7%（MAE=0.398 vs 1.162），响应满意度在5点李克特量表上提升了17.52%（从3.71到4.36）。

Conclusion: 融合语义和行为线索的查询级专业知识推断能有效提升LLM个性化响应的准确性，优于仅依赖静态画像或纯文本的基线方法。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used by end users, yet existing personalization methods relying on static profiles or text-only signals fail to capture query-specific expertise variation. We present ExPerT, a query-wise personalization framework that adapts LLM responses to users' query domain expertise by combining semantic and behavioral cues. ExPerT consists of two key components: (i) a semantic-behavioral expertise inference module that jointly interprets query text and keystroke dynamics via in-context LLM prompting, and (ii) an expertise-conditioned response generation that adapts the level of detail, terminology, and conceptual complexity. Our user study with 40 participants and 1270 queries demonstrated that ExPerT reduced expertise inference error by 65.7% compared to the strongest baseline (MAE = 0.398 vs. 1.162) and improved response satisfaction by 17.52% (from 3.71 to 4.36) on a 5-point Likert scale.

</details>


### [149] [Mitigating Confirmation Bias through Hand-Drawing Videos](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01359)
*Chenyu Lin,Cindy Xiong,Icy Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了通过手绘动态视频展示数据可视化构建过程，是否能减轻确认偏见并提高数据解读准确性。实验表明，相比静态图表，手绘视频帮助参与者更准确地解读数据，尤其是当数据与个人先验信念冲突时，减少了信念一致性错误，增加了信念覆盖反应。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据可视化解读常受观察者先验信念的影响，产生确认偏见，导致决策偏差。现有研究较少探索可视化呈现方式本身如何从认知层面缓解这一偏见。

Method: 开展对照实验，比较静态条形图与手绘动态构建视频两种呈现方式，实验设置与参与者先验信念一致或冲突的不同上下文条件。

Result: 手绘视频显著提升了数据解读准确性，尤其在数据与信念冲突时；减少了信念一致性错误；增加了信念覆盖（即依据数据纠正原有信念）的反应频率。

Conclusion: 暴露可视化的构建过程有助于更准确的推理，减轻确认偏见的影响，为设计偏见缓解型数据界面提供了新的设计空间。

Abstract: Understanding data visualizations is essential for informed decision-making, yet interpretation is often shaped and even distorted by prior beliefs. We investigate whether an embodied pedagogical approach, in which viewers observe the dynamic hand-drawing of a visualization, can mitigate confirmation bias and improve interpretation accuracy. We conducted a study comparing static bar charts to videos in which charts are constructed through hand-drawing, across contexts that either align with or challenge participants' prior beliefs. The results indicate that hand-drawn videos helped participants accurately interpret data, even when the data conflicted with their prior beliefs. This approach also reduced belief-consistent errors and increased belief-overriding responses. These findings suggest that exposing the construction process of a visualization supports more accurate reasoning and mitigates the influence of confirmation bias. Consequently, this work introduces a promising design space for bias-mitigating data interfaces.

</details>


### [150] [Mind the Trust Gap: Identifying (Mis)alignments in Teacher-Student Views Toward Control and Agency in K-12 Classroom AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01506)
*Tomohiro Nagashima,Lisa Siegrist,Niklas Scholz,Shintaro Sato,Martina Vincoli,Man Su*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过对16名K-12学生和15名教师进行故事板速配研究，发现师生在AI决策控制信任度、社会情感学习等方面存在观点错位，并揭示了师生关系对AI互动的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究未充分揭示教师与学生在K-12课堂中对AI决策控制权的看法是否一致，尤其缺乏对多方利益相关者观点对齐/错位的深入理解。

Method: 在德国开展故事板驱动的速配研究，招募16名K-12学生和15名教师，通过明确的对偶匹配分析比较师生在AI决策控制权上的观点异同。

Result: 师生在AI信任度、学生社会情感学习方面存在显著观点错位；师生关系（非AI使用场景）对AI互动中的观点和交互具有重要塑造作用。

Conclusion: 师生-AI交互中的偏好具有复杂性，取决于利益相关者之间的动态关系；需针对观点差距制定弥合策略。

Abstract: As Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based technologies have been integrated into school classrooms where multiple stakeholders (with different roles) interact with each other, it is critical to deeply understand stakeholder views in the classroom. In particular, prior work has not fully uncovered how teachers' and school students' views might or might not align well with each other, especially in K-12 classrooms. We conducted a speed-dating study using storyboards with 16 school students and 15 school teachers in Germany to investigate alignments and misalignments between their views on student-AI decision-making control in K-12 classroom. Through an explicit pair-matching analysis, we found that students and teachers had misaligned views on several key topics, including how much they trust AI and social and emotional aspects of student learning with AI. Findings also revealed the importance of teacher-student relationships outside of AI use that shape stakeholders' views and interactions. We discuss potential reasons for the observed misaligned views and strategies to fill the perspective gaps. This study illustrates the complexities of preferences in teacher-student-AI interactions that depend on the dynamic relations among the stakeholders.

</details>


### [151] [OrchestrXR: A Multi-Agent System for Idea-to-Prototype XR Study Authoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01588)
*Shuqi Liao,Chenfei Zhu,Karthik Ramani,Voicu Popescu*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: OrchestrXR是一个多智能体人机协同工作流，通过结构化模式、多智能体编排和交互式人机界面，帮助研究人员将早期的XR研究想法转化为可运行的Unity原型，涵盖研究设计、场景生成和交互生成三个阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的XR研究从想法到可运行原型的过程涉及研究设计、场景构建和交互实现等多个环节，但这些环节之间是碎片化的，缺乏统一的工作流支持，导致早期原型开发效率低下。

Method: 提出OrchestrXR多智能体人机协作工作流，通过结构化模式（structured schemas）、多智能体编排（multi-agent orchestration）和交互式人机界面，将XR研究创建分解为研究设计、场景生成和交互生成三个阶段，支持可控的工作流，最终输出Unity原型。

Result: 与12名XR研究人员的用户研究表明，OrchestrXR能为早期XR研究创作提供有效支持，并在各个阶段保持强烈的意图一致性。

Conclusion: OrchestrXR验证了多智能体协作工作流在XR研究早期原型开发中的有效性，能够弥合创意构思与可运行原型之间的鸿沟。

Abstract: Extended Reality (XR) has become an important interaction paradigm in Human-Computer Interaction (HCI). XR studies are used to investigate interaction, perception, and user behavior in immersive environments, and typically involve experimental tasks, 3D scenes, and interactive logic. However, turning an initial XR study idea into a runnable prototype remains fragmented across study design, scene construction, and interaction implementation. We present OrchestrXR, a multi-agent human-AI workflow for early-stage idea-to-prototype XR study authoring. Rather than treating XR study creation as one-shot generation, OrchestrXR supports a controllable workflow across study design, scene generation, and interaction generation through structured schemas, multi-agent orchestration, and interactive human-agent interfaces, producing a Unity-based prototype from a researcher's idea. A user study with 12 XR researchers suggests that OrchestrXR provides effective support for early-stage XR study authoring with strong intent preservation across stages.

</details>


### [152] [Made to Feel: How Designers Bring Emotions into Affective Visualization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01593)
*Yixin Bai,Ziyi Wang,Keke Wu,Fumeng Yang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 通过对15位可视化从业者的半结构化访谈，该研究揭示了设计者如何在可视化作品中融入情感：识别了情感的三种功能（入口、参与、结果）、情感设计的三个层面（数据、设计、受众）及相应策略、伦理考量，并发现情感意图往往在设计过程中涌现而非预先规划，情感影响来自累积的设计选择而非孤立元素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感可视化在研究中日益受到关注，但设计者如何在可视化工作中融入情感这一过程尚未被探索。

Method: 对15位可视化从业者进行半结构化访谈，采用混合主题分析方法。

Result: (1) 情感对观众具有三种功能：入口、参与、结果；(2) 设计者处理情感的三个层面：数据、设计、受众及其对应设计策略；(3) 设计过程中的伦理考量。此外发现情感意图常在过程中涌现，情感影响来自累积设计选择。

Conclusion: 情感设计是迭代涌现的过程而非预先规划；评估是参与者识别出的关键挑战。

Abstract: Affective visualization is increasingly studied in visualization research, yet how designers bring emotions into their visualization work remains unexplored. This paper addresses this gap through semi-structured interviews with 15 visualization practitioners. Using hybrid thematic analysis, we identify: (1) three functions that emotions can serve for viewers (entry, engagement, outcome); (2) three facets of how designers work with emotion (data, design, audience), along with design strategies; and (3) ethical considerations in the design process. We also observe that affective intent often emerges during the design process rather than being planned from the outset, and that emotional impact arises from accumulated design choices rather than isolated visual elements. Finally, we highlight evaluation as a key challenge identified by our participants.

</details>


### [153] [Evaluating Glanceable Multi-Device Family Health Tracking with Smartwatches and Home Displays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01618)
*Lucas M. Silva,Evropi Stefanidi,Aehong Min,Franceli L. Cibrian,Jesus A. Beltran,Cassie Zeiler,Sabrina E. B. Schuck,Kimberley D. Lakes,Gillian R. Hayes,Daniel A. Epstein*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 本研究通过9周家庭实验（12个家庭44人），比较了仅智能手表、仅家庭显示屏以及二者组合三种设计对家庭情绪与目标追踪的影响。发现组合设计显著提升追踪频率，儿童比成人更稳定参与，多设备冗余适应家庭成员的多样化需求。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有普适计算研究关注个人健康追踪设备，但对多设备支持家庭健康信息学（即健康管理的协作性）的理解不足。本文旨在探究家庭如何采用和感知跨情境共享健康数据的无障碍访问。

Method: 44人（12个家庭）在九周内交替使用三种设计：仅智能手表、仅家庭显示屏、二者组合。通过日志分析追踪行为频率，并结合访谈分析用户感知。

Result: 有家庭显示屏时情绪和目标追踪频率显著高于仅智能手表；整体情绪追踪随时间下降；追踪高峰在下午，周末下降，家中追踪频率是其他场景的2.6倍；儿童比成人更稳定地参与。访谈显示智能手表支持分散时的机会性追踪和感知，显示屏促进家庭协作和提醒，多设备冗余适应不同成员的偏好与习惯。

Conclusion: 多设备家庭健康信息学应支持包容性、可一瞥式、适应性强的数据共享，以容纳不同家庭成员的多样化偏好和日常习惯。

Abstract: While ubiquitous computing research has explored diverse devices for personal health tracking, we know less about multi-device designs for family informatics, where health management is inherently collaborative. To understand how families adopt and perceive ubiquitous access to shared health data across contexts, we evaluated smartwatch-only, home display-only, and combined designs for tracking moods and goals, domains central to family health behavior regulation. 44 people across 12 families alternated between these designs over nine weeks. Log analysis revealed that mood tracking and goal reporting were significantly more frequent with the home display present compared to smartwatch-only use, despite an overall decline in mood tracking over time. Tracking peaked in afternoons, dropped on weekends, and occurred 2.6X more at home, with children tracking more consistently than adults across all designs. From interview analysis, we learned how family data glanceability on smartwatches supported opportunistic tracking and awareness while apart, whereas displays reminded families to self-track and collaborate during home routines including members that avoided wearables (e.g., non-participants). Multi-device redundancy accommodated diversity in routines, mobility patterns, and device preferences among members in the same family. We discuss opportunities for multi-device family informatics that accommodates different preferences through inclusive, glanceable, and adaptable ubiquitous data sharing.

</details>


### [154] [From Answer Generators to Reasoning Facilitators: Designing AI Tutors for Mathematical Reasoning in High-Stakes Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01692)
*Yuming Feng,Yuan Tian,Erica Zhao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AITutor系统通过将教学理论转化为界面功能，帮助初中生在高压备考环境下进行数学推理修复，研究发现学生抵制传统苏格拉底式对话而采用"答案优先"策略，并提出了"以推理为中心的产品循环"设计框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型语言模型快速融入教育技术可能导致数学学习退化为简单的答案生成，缺乏对推理过程的真正支持。研究者希望设计一个交互系统，将理论教学机制转化为具体的用户界面功能，以在真实的高压备考环境下支持学生的数学推理。

Method: 采用混合方法三角验证：7,379个遥测事件、8次情境观察、10次访谈。通过生成性研究、可用性研究及12名初中生参与的中考备考现场部署实验，评估AITutor系统的交互效果。

Result: 学生在时间压力下主动抵制传统的苏格拉底式对话，将"答案优先"的快捷方式重新利用为关键的诊断检查点。分层工作示例、步骤链接视觉锚定和元认知支架等功能能够降低推理修复的交互成本。

Conclusion: 提出了"以推理为中心的产品循环"（Reasoning-Centered Product Loop），为设计能够结构性支持推理检查、局部修复、课程验证和延迟检索的AI系统提供了可操作的启示。

Abstract: The rapid integration of Large Language Models (LLMs) into educational technology threatens to reduce mathematical learning to mere answer generation. This paper presents a generative study, usability study, and 12-participant field deployment of AITutor, an interactive system that translates theoretical pedagogical mechanisms into concrete user interface features. We explore how junior-high students preparing for high-stakes exams (Zhongkao) interact with AI tutoring. Through mixed-methods triangulation (7,379 telemetry events, 8 contextual observations, 10 interviews), we reveal that students actively resist traditional Socratic dialogue under time pressure, repurposing "answer-first" shortcuts as vital diagnostic checkpoints. We demonstrate how features like layered worked examples, step-linked visual grounding, and metacognitive scaffolding lower the interaction cost of reasoning repair. We contribute a "Reasoning-Centered Product Loop," offering actionable implications for designing AI that structurally supports the inspection, local repair, curriculum verification, and delayed retrieval of mathematical reasoning in the wild.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [155] [WaveLander: A Generalizable Hierarchical Control Framework for UAV Landing on Wave-Disturbed Platforms via Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01281)
*Chun-Kit Li,Iok Long Sit,Ming Fung Siu,Ka Yu Kui,Hin Wang Lin,Pengyu Wang,Ling Shi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出WaveLander，一种通过强化学习实现的分层控制框架，用于无人机在波浪扰动海洋平台上的自主降落。RL策略将平台相对观测映射为垂直速度参考值，由底层飞行控制器维持姿态稳定，将动态平台降落简化为低维时序感知控制问题，在随机波浪扰动下展现出鲁棒性和泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无人机在波浪扰动的海洋平台上自主降落面临平台随机运动、姿态时变及触地条件不确定等挑战，现有基于模型的方法需要精确运动预测和在线优化，而端到端学习方法训练复杂度高且可解释性有限。

Method: 采用分层控制框架：上层RL策略将平台相对观测映射为标量垂直速度参考值，下层传统飞行控制器维持姿态稳定和横向跟踪，将动态平台降落降维为低维、时序感知的控制问题。

Result: 在随机波浪诱导的平台运动仿真下，WaveLander实现了鲁棒的降落性能，并能够泛化到未见过的扰动条件。

Conclusion: 分层学习控制方法为海洋平台无人机回收展现了潜力，无需显式切换规则即可实现平滑降落行为。

Abstract: Autonomous landing of unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) on wave-disturbed marine platforms remains challenging due to stochastic platform motion, time-varying platform attitude, and uncertain touchdown conditions. Existing model-based methods often require accurate motion prediction and online optimization, while end-to-end learning approaches may suffer from high training complexity and limited interpretability. This paper presents WaveLander, a hierarchical control framework via reinforcement learning (RL) that decouples vertical landing decision-making from low-level flight stabilization. The RL policy maps a compact platform-relative observation to a scalar vertical velocity reference, while a conventional low-level flight controller maintains attitude stability and lateral tracking. This formulation reduces dynamic platform landing to a low-dimensional, timing-aware control problem and enables smooth landing behavior without explicit switching rules. Simulation results under randomized wave-induced platform motions show that WaveLander achieves robust landing performance and generalizes to unseen disturbance conditions, demonstrating the potential of hierarchical learning-based control for marine UAV recovery.

</details>


### [156] [Adaptive Companionship for Group-Following Robots: Handling Dynamically Changing Group Formations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01287)
*Cong-Thanh Vu,Yen-Chen Liu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉语言模型(VLM)的社交机器人自适应群体陪伴方法，利用VLM的语义推理能力推断陪伴位置、维持社交距离并理解群体动态，结合MPPI控制器确保安全稳定。在五个场景中，该方法相比基线方法成功率提升15%，碰撞率降低25%，用户研究也表明陪伴行为自然且社交适当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 人类群体通常不遵循固定队形，这给机器人维持自然陪伴行为带来了重大挑战。发展类人社交认知需要机器人能够自然地陪伴人类群体。

Method: 首先检测群体成员，感知模块生成交互群体空间的视觉表示作为VLM输入，然后将VLM的语义推理与模型预测路径积分(MPPI)控制器相结合，以推断陪伴位置、维持社交距离并理解群体动态，确保稳定性和安全性。

Result: 在五个场景的实验评估中，该方法相比基线方法成功率提升15%，碰撞率降低25%。用户研究表明生成的陪伴行为被认为是自然且社交适当的。

Conclusion: 基于VLM的自适应群体陪伴方法能够有效实现机器人对人类群体的自然陪伴，在成功率和安全性方面均优于基线方法，且被用户感知为自然和社交适当。

Abstract: Accompanying a group of humans is an essential aspect of developing human-like social cognition in robots. However, human groups typically do not follow fixed formations, which poses significant challenges for robots in maintaining natural companionship behaviors. In this paper, we propose an adaptive group-accompaniment method for social robots based on Vision-Language Models (VLMs), leveraging their semantic reasoning capabilities to infer companion positions, maintain social distances, and understand group dynamics. The members of the group are first detected, and a perceptual module generates visual representations of the interaction group space as input to the VLM, which is then combined with a Model Predictive Path Integral (MPPI) controller to ensure stability and safety. Experimental evaluations across five scenarios show that the proposed method enables robots to accompany the group effectively, demonstrating a 15\% improvement in success rate and a 25\% reduction in collision rate compared to baseline approaches. Additionally, a user study indicates that the generated companionship behaviors are perceived as natural and socially appropriate.

</details>


### [157] [The Three Dimensions of ROS 2 Middleware](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01304)
*Sanghoon Lee,Taehun Kim,Angelo Corsaro,Kyung-Joon Park*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文系统综述了ROS 2中间件架构，从空间、时间、状态三个结构维度分析了其在动态无线环境下的局限性，揭示了结构权衡关系，并提出了鲁棒可扩展机器人中间件架构的研究路线图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ROS 2中间件（如DDS和Zenoh）在动态和资源受限的无线环境下表现出结构性局限，但缺乏系统性的分析框架来理解这些局限及其相互权衡。

Method: 提出一个概念性框架，从Space（空间抽象）、Time（时间可预测性）、State（状态连续性）三个维度系统分析ROS 2中间件架构，并通过综述现有实现和先前研究来梳理各维度间的结构权衡。

Result: 发现三个关键结构权衡：1) 空间抽象会掩盖网络变异性并削弱时间保证；2) 状态连续性机制引入的计算和网络开销与时延关键通信竞争；3) 这些权衡表征了当代机器人中间件的实践极限。

Conclusion: 通过综合架构模式并识别当前建模和分析方法的空白，本文为鲁棒可扩展的机器人中间件架构提供了原则性的研究路线图。

Abstract: ROS 2 (Robot Operating System 2) has emerged as the de facto standard for modern robot software development, with middleware implementations such as the Data Distribution Service (DDS) and Zenoh forming the core infrastructure for distributed robotic communication. Despite their architectural flexibility, these middleware systems exhibit structural limitations, particularly under dynamic and resource-constrained wireless environments. This paper presents a systematic survey of ROS 2 middleware and introduces a conceptual framework to examine its architectural limits through three structural dimensions required by distributed robotic systems, namely Space, Time, and State. We first provide a structured analysis of middleware architecture and operational dynamics, including discovery, data exchange, and state management mechanisms. Building on this foundation, we formalize Time as temporal predictability for control loops, Space as spatial abstraction from physical topology to enable modular deployment, and State as contextual continuity despite dynamic node participation and intermittent connectivity. Through a comprehensive review of existing implementations and prior studies, we organize middleware research according to the structural trade-offs that arise among these dimensions. Under constrained wireless conditions, spatial abstraction can obscure network variability and weaken temporal guarantees, while mechanisms that preserve state continuity introduce computational and network overhead that competes with time-critical communication. These interactions reveal structural trade-offs that characterize the practical limits of contemporary robot middleware. By synthesizing architectural patterns and identifying gaps in current modeling and analysis approaches, this survey outlines a principled research roadmap for robust and scalable robotic middleware architectures.

</details>


### [158] [Neuro-Symbolic Safety Guidance for Vision-Language-Action Models via Constrained Flow Matching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01378)
*William English,Hao Zheng,Rickard Ewetz*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种神经符号安全引导机制，用于基于流匹配的VLA模型，通过在去噪过程中纠正轨迹预测中的安全违规，实现预测性碰撞避免，在SafeLIBERO基准上分别达到82.8%的碰撞避免率和81.6%的任务成功率，较单步方法提升6.3%和19.8%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型的安全措施仅能防止机器人下一步动作引起的碰撞，缺乏对预测性碰撞风险的防范能力，限制了其在现实世界的部署。

Method: 将安全强制执行建模为最小范数约束优化问题，在流匹配迭代去噪过程中对中间噪声轨迹预测进行安全违规纠正，实现符号约束满足与神经轨迹生成的交错。

Result: 在SafeLIBERO基准上，碰撞避免率达82.8%，任务成功率达81.6%，分别比单步方法提升6.3%和19.8%，在长 horizon 任务上增益最大。

Conclusion: 该方法通过预测性而非反应式的碰撞避免，有效提升了VLA模型在真实部署中的安全性，尤其在分布偏移累积的长周期任务中表现显著。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have demonstrated promising generalization capabilities across robotic manipulation tasks, yet their real-world deployment remains limited by the lack of effective safety measures. Specifically, existing safety measures only prevent collisions caused by the robot's next action. In this paper, we propose a neuro-symbolic safety guidance mechanism for flow matching based VLAs that enables predictive collision avoidance. Flow matching based VLAs determine the next actions by predicting a trajectory (a sequence of actions) through an iterative neural flow matching process. Our method formulates safety enforcement as a minimum-norm constrained optimization problem that corrects safety violations during the denoising process of noisy intermediate trajectory predictions. By analyzing predicted trajectories and applying corrections during iterative denoising, our approach anticipates collisions before they become unavoidable. This interleaving of symbolic constraint satisfaction with neural trajectory generation enables predictive collision avoidance rather than reactive intervention. On the SafeLIBERO benchmark, our method achieves 82.8% collision avoidance and 81.6% task success, a 6.3% and 19.8% improvement respectively over single-step methods, with the largest gains on long-horizon tasks where compounding distribution shift is most pronounced. Video demonstrations of our approach are included on our project page at https://willenglish.tech/SafetyGuidedFlowMatching/.

</details>


### [159] [Choreographing the Way of Water: A Computational Framework for Aquatic Robotic Art](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02174)
*Aswin Ramachandran,Christopher Golling,Sebastian Burmester,Noa Sendlhofer,Jan Kamm,Ruiheng Jiang,Raffaello D'Andrea*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了Way of Water系统，这是一个将自主水面舰艇舰队作为分布式编舞平台的网络物理架构。核心贡献是Way of Water Studio——一个基于浏览器的、时间线合成的编舞工具，将舰队视为类似DAW的乐器，通过Sequential Convex Programming和Model Predictive Control实现轨迹生成与扰动抑制，并经过苏黎世湖18艘船的《天鹅湖》和威尼斯双年展8艘船的演示验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开阔水域的机器人编舞受非线性流体动力学支配，环境扰动和非线性系统动力学带来了重大挑战。现有范式局限于二维水面像素表达，缺乏将机器人舰队作为三维体积域表达媒介的工具，且高性能水机器人技术对非程序员艺术家而言门槛过高。

Method: 提出Way of Water Studio浏览器端时间线合成编舞范式，将舰队视为类DAW乐器；使用层流喷嘴和多区域照明将表达范围从2D水面扩展到3D体积域；集成Sequential Convex Programming进行轨迹生成，Model Predictive Control进行扰动抑制；构建完整网络物理栈：定制全向底盘、水域状态估计与控制栈、LTE/MQTT舰队链路及RTK-GPS时间同步。

Result: 在苏黎世湖18艘船《天鹅湖》演出和威尼斯双年展8艘船演示两个独立场景中完成系统验证。

Conclusion: 为流体机器人蜂群的设计与部署建立了基础参考，证明通过时间线编舞范式可以降低非程序员艺术家使用高性能水机器人技术的门槛。

Abstract: Robotic choreography in open water is governed by nonlinear fluid dynamics, which impose significant challenges due to environmental disturbances and nonlinear system dynamics. This paper presents the cyber-physical architecture of Way of Water, a vertically integrated framework that orchestrates a fleet of autonomous surface vessels as a distributed choreographic platform. Moving beyond the surface-pixel paradigm, these vessels use laminar nozzles and multi-zone lighting to extend their expressive range from the 2D water plane into the 3D volumetric domain. Our primary contribution is the Way of Water Studio, a browser-based, timeline-compositing authoring paradigm that treats the fleet as a DAW-like instrument for music-responsive choreography. The Studio encapsulates Sequential Convex Programming for trajectory generation and Model Predictive Control for disturbance rejection presented through a visual timeline, broadening access to high-performance aquatic robotics for non-programmer artists. Grounding the Studio is the full cyber-physical stack: a custom holonomic chassis, a state-estimation and control stack tuned for the aquatic domain, and an LTE/MQTT fleet link with RTK-GPS time synchronization. We report on the system's validation across two distinct deployments: an 18-vessel Swan Lake interpretation at Lake Zurich and an 8-vessel Time Space Existence 2025 Venice Biennale demonstration at Forte Marghera, establishing a foundational reference for the design and deployment of fluidic robotic swarms.

</details>


### [160] [A Stereo Visual SLAM System Using Object-Level Motion Estimation and Geometric Filtering Based on Cross Disparity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02005)
*Sujan Kumar Dhali,Bhaskar Dasgupta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出OCD SLAM，一种动态立体视觉SLAM框架，通过联合处理场景中的动态物体和动态特征，在ORB-SLAM2基础上扩展，利用视差与"交叉视差"的几何不一致性检测动态特征点，并集成3D目标检测与卡尔曼滤波跟踪进行物体级运动分类，在KITTI数据集上显著提升了轨迹精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统视觉SLAM系统在动态环境中因静态世界假设而导致位姿估计和建图失败，现有方法难以同时有效处理动态物体和动态特征点的干扰。

Method: 提出交叉视差（cross disparity）这一新概念，利用时间与立体不一致性进行特征级运动分析；集成SMOKE 3D目标检测模块与卡尔曼滤波物体跟踪，进行物体级运动分类；通过特征级与物体级联合分析实现动态场景元素的鲁棒分离。

Result: 在KITTI Odometry和KITTI Raw数据集上评估，OCD SLAM相比ORB-SLAM2及多种最先进动态SLAM方法在轨迹精度上取得显著提升；消融实验验证了交叉视差模块的有效性，能检测到3D目标检测遗漏的动态特征。

Conclusion: 提出的几何与学习相结合的动态SLAM框架能够有效处理动态环境，交叉视差机制与物体级运动分类互为补充，共同提升位姿估计的鲁棒性和精度。

Abstract: This paper presents OCD SLAM, a dynamic stereo visual SLAM framework that extends ORB-SLAM2 by jointly addressing dynamic objects and dynamic features in the scene. Usual visual SLAM systems operating in dynamic environments often fail in the presence of moving objects, due to the static-world assumption used in pose estimation and mapping. To address this predicament, we introduce a novel geometric approach based on the discrepancy between disparity and a newly proposed notion called ``cross disparity'', which exploits both temporal and stereo inconsistency to identify dynamic feature points. Complementary to this feature-level motion analysis, OCD SLAM integrates a 3D object detection module (SMOKE) with Kalman filter-based object tracking to perform object-level motion classification, enabling robust separation of static and dynamic scene elements for accurate pose estimation. The proposed approach has been evaluated on various sequences from the KITTI Odometry and KITTI Raw datasets. Results demonstrate that OCD SLAM achieves significant improvement in trajectory accuracy compared to ORB-SLAM2 and several state-of-the-art dynamic SLAM methods. Ablation studies further demonstrate the effectiveness of the cross disparity module in the KITTI Raw dataset and show that this method is able to detect dynamic features that are missed by the 3D object detection scheme alone.

</details>


### [161] [Actuator Reality Shaping for Zero-Shot Sim-to-Real Robot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02205)
*Satoshi Yamamori,Koji Ishihara,Kentaro Minamikawa,Kiyoharu Ohomori,Taiyo Yazaki,Norikazu Sugimoto,Jun Morimoto*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为"执行器真实感塑造"（Actuator Reality Shaping）的新范式，通过设计二自由度前馈-反馈控制器使物理执行器的闭环行为匹配仿真中使用的理想二阶参考动力学，从而实现强化学习策略从仿真到真实机器人的零样本迁移，无需调整仿真器或进行任务级微调。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习中的Sim-to-real迁移常因仿真中假设的理想执行器动力学与物理电机非线性、硬件依赖行为之间的差异而受限。现有方法通过增加仿真保真度来弥合这一差距，但作者提出了一种替代思路：不修改仿真器去匹配真实世界，而是反过来塑造真实执行器的行为去匹配仿真。

Method: 为每个关节配备一个二自由度（2-DoF）前馈-反馈控制器，将参考响应塑造与鲁棒镇定解耦。前馈部分负责匹配理想的二阶参考动力学，反馈部分负责鲁棒镇定。这样为强化学习策略提供了一个标准化的执行器接口，策略仅需基于预设的参考模型进行训练。

Result: 在单关节高减速比伺服电机（带外部负载）、7自由度机械臂到达任务、轮腿机器人斜坡行驶以及人形机器人行走等多个平台上进行了验证。与标准伺服控制和代表性的real-to-sim-to-real基线方法相比，该方法显著降低了sim-to-real跟踪误差，提升了零样本任务性能。

Conclusion: 执行器真实感塑造可以作为一种可复用的接口，用于跨多种硬件平台的机器人学习，实现了无需任务级微调或学习执行器模型的零样本迁移。

Abstract: Sim-to-real transfer in robot learning is often limited by discrepancies between the ideal actuator dynamics assumed during policy training and the nonlinear, hardware-dependent behavior of physical motors. While conventional approaches attempt to bridge this gap by increasing simulator fidelity through system identification, domain randomization, or learned actuator models, we introduce an alternative paradigm: actuator reality shaping. Instead of modifying the simulator to match the real world, our method shapes the closed-loop behavior of physical actuators to match the idealized second-order reference dynamics used in simulation. By equipping each joint with a two-degree-of-freedom feedforward--feedback controller, we decouple reference-response shaping from robust stabilization, thereby providing a standardized actuator interface for reinforcement learning policies. As a result, policies trained only with the prescribed reference model can be deployed zero-shot on real hardware without task-level fine-tuning or learned actuator models. We validate the approach on a single-joint high-gear-ratio servo under external loads and a 7-DOF robotic arm reaching task, where actuator reality shaping substantially reduces sim-to-real tracking error and improves zero-shot task performance compared with standard servo-control and representative real-to-sim-to-real baselines. We further demonstrate zero-shot transfer on a wheeled-legged robot driving over a slope and a humanoid robot walking, suggesting that actuator reality shaping can serve as a reusable interface for robot learning across diverse hardware platforms.

</details>


### [162] [CoFL-S: Spatially Queryable Sector Flow Fields for Local Language-Conditioned Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02222)
*Haokun Liu,Zhaoqi Ma,Yicheng Chen,Wentao Zhang,Masaki Kitagawa,Zicen Xiong,Jinjie Li,Moju Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出CoFL-S低层级视觉-语言-动作框架，通过预测语言条件流场生成连续轨迹，在连续时间Habitat基准测试中优于动作词元和动作分块基线，并实现零样本真实世界部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN领域过度关注高层指令推理、记忆和全局地图构建，而低层动作表示（low-level action representation）相对未被充分探索。

Method: CoFL-S框架：1) 预测语言条件下的局部可见区域流场；2) 通过滚动预测的流场生成连续轨迹；3) 将VLN-CE整体episode转换为帧级局部监督（包含子指令、动作、轨迹、密集流场目标）；4) 提出连续时间Habitat基准，通过共享速度命令控制器隔离低层动作接口。

Result: 在匹配编码器和训练设置下，CoFL-S在不同规划器频率的连续时间Habitat基准中一致优于动作词元和动作分块基线；零样本真实世界闭环部署也展现出优势。

Conclusion: 低层动作表示是VLN中值得关注的研究方向，基于条件流场的连续轨迹生成方法在仿真和真实场景中均有效。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation has increasingly emphasized high-level instruction reasoning, memory, global map construction, and instruction decomposition, while the low-level action representation remains comparatively underexplored. We propose CoFL-S, a low-level vision-language-action framework that predicts a language-conditioned flow field over the robot's local visible sector and generates continuous trajectories by rolling out the predicted field. To train this low-level representation, we convert each VLN-CE episode, originally a whole-episode instruction paired with an action sequence, into frame-level local supervision with aligned sub-instructions and matched action, trajectory, and dense flow-field targets. For evaluation, we introduce a continuous-time Habitat benchmark that isolates low-level action interfaces from instruction decomposition and executes all methods through a shared velocity-command controller, enabling decomposition-independent closed-loop comparison across different planner frequencies rather than fixed discrete forward-and-turn transitions in VLN-CE. Under matched encoders and training settings, CoFL-S consistently outperforms action-token and action-chunk baselines across planner frequencies in the continuous-time Habitat benchmark, and zero-shot real-world closed-loop deployment further shows its advantage over both baselines beyond simulation.

</details>


### [163] [NEUROSYMLAND: Neuro-Symbolic Landing-Site Assessment for Robust and Edge-Deployable UAV Autonomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02277)
*Weixian Qian,Tianyi Yang,Sebastian Schroder,Yao Deng,Jiaohong Yao,Xiao Cheng,Richard Han,Xi Zheng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: NEUROSYMLAND 是一种面向边缘无人机平台的神经符号着陆点评估系统，结合轻量级感知与显式符号安全推理，在72个模拟场景中取得61次成功评估，优于四个基线方法（37-57次成功），并通过100次硬件在环实验验证了边缘部署的可行性与可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于纯视觉学习的自主无人机着陆点评估方法在地形变化下性能退化严重，且安全决策缺乏可解释性，亟需一种兼具鲁棒性与透明性的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 从机载视觉输入构建概率语义场景图（PSSG），利用符号约束（地形平坦度、障碍物间距、空间一致性）对候选着陆区域进行结构化推理，在感知不确定性下实现安全评估，同时保持边缘设备可执行的计算开销。

Result: 在72个模拟场景中取得61次成功评估，优于四个基线（37-57次）；100次硬件在环实验验证了端到端延迟、CPU/GPU利用率、内存占用和功耗等系统级指标，符号推理仅占延迟的极小部分。

Conclusion: NEUROSYMLAND 在边缘受限的无人机硬件上实现了鲁棒且可解释的着陆点评估，符号推理开销极低，主要计算瓶颈在感知与PSSG构建环节，相关代码与数据已开源。

Abstract: Safe landing-site assessment in unstructured environments remains a key challenge for autonomous UAV deployment, as vision-only learning approaches often degrade under terrain variability and provide limited transparency in safety decisions. We present NEUROSYMLAND, a neuro-symbolic landing-site assessment system that integrates lightweight perception with explicit safety reasoning. The framework constructs a probabilistic semantic scene graph from onboard visual input and evaluates candidate landing regions using symbolic constraints capturing terrain flatness, obstacle clearance, and spatial consistency, enabling structured reasoning under perceptual uncertainty while maintaining edge-feasible execution. Across 72 simulated landing scenarios spanning diverse terrains, NEUROSYMLAND achieves 61 successful assessments, outperforming four competitive baselines (37-57 successes). To evaluate deployability, we further conduct 100 hardware-in-the-loop trials with randomized initial poses, profiling end-to-end latency, stage-wise execution time, and system-level metrics including CPU/GPU utilization, memory footprint, and power consumption. Results demonstrate improved robustness and interpretability with bounded edge-resource usage. Profiling shows that symbolic reasoning contributes only a small fraction of end-to-end latency, while the main computational cost arises from perception and PSSG construction. These results demonstrate the feasibility of deploying the landing-site assessment stack on edge-constrained UAV hardware, and all source code, datasets, prompts, and symbolic rule refinement examples are released in an open-source repository

</details>


### [164] [Real-Time Visual Intelligence on Low-Cost UAVs: A Modular Approach for Tracking, Scanning, and Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02298)
*Andrei-Marian Ungureanu,Stelian Spînu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文基于DJI Tello无人机平台，开发了一套集成人脸检测、人脸识别和单目深度估计的智能个人助理无人机系统，采用模块化架构、网页交互界面和轻量级神经网络模型，在低成本硬件上实现了人员跟踪、室内扫描和虚拟传感器自主线跟随等实时功能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有商业无人机解决方案成本高昂且不开放，本研究旨在利用低成本硬件和开源技术，构建一个可访问的智能无人机个人助理系统，验证高级AI技术在资源受限平台上实时部署的可行性。

Method: 采用DJI Tello无人机平台，设计模块化架构集成三种核心AI功能（人脸检测、人脸识别、单目深度估计）；通过网页界面实现无人机控制与实时视频监控；使用Python服务端处理视觉数据，并采用针对嵌入式系统优化的轻量级神经网络模型进行推理。

Result: 系统在真实世界条件下表现出稳健性能，成功实现了人员跟踪、室内扫描以及基于虚拟传感器的自主线跟随功能。

Conclusion: 本项目验证了先进AI技术在实时机器人系统中的适用性，证明了在受限硬件上部署此类技术的可行性，为未来军事、救援和监视任务中的自主无人机研究奠定了基础。

Abstract: Autonomous drones are rapidly transforming modern warfare and civil applications alike. This paper presents the development of an integrated intelligent drone system designed to serve as a personal assistant. Leveraging the DJI Tello drone platform, we implemented a modular architecture that integrates three core artificial intelligence functionalities: facial detection, facial recognition, and depth estimation from monocular vision. A web-based interface enables seamless drone control and real-time video monitoring, while a Python-based server processes visual data and executes inference pipelines using lightweight neural models optimized for embedded systems. Unlike existing commercial solutions, this system emphasizes accessibility, low-cost hardware, and open-source technologies. The system demonstrates robust performance in real-world conditions, including person tracking, indoor scanning, and autonomous line following using virtual sensors. This project validates the applicability of advanced AI techniques in real-time robotic systems and illustrates the feasibility of deploying them on constrained hardware, providing a foundation for future research in autonomous UAVs for military, rescue, and surveillance missions.

</details>


### [165] [The Moving Eye: Enhancing VLA Spatial Generalization via Hybrid Dynamic Data Collection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02322)
*Jincheng Tang,Yilong Zhu,Zhengyuan Xie,Jiang-Jiang Liu,Jiaxing Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据中心的解决方案，通过混合连续相机运动与多样静态视角的策略（双机械臂设置），有效减少了视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型中的捷径学习（Shortcut Learning）问题，显著提升了机器人的空间泛化能力。实验表明，该策略优于单纯增加静态视角，且对多种架构（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均有效。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型在通用机器人操作中表现优异，但空间泛化能力脆弱。仅增加视角数量不足以解决问题，模型容易陷入捷径学习——依赖物体间或相机与机器人基座之间的固定相对位姿等虚假相关性，而非学习真正的空间关系。

Method: 采用双机械臂设置：一支臂执行操作任务，另一支臂作为移动环境相机。系统评估三种数据分布模式：固定视角（Fixed）、多固定视角（Multi-Fixed）和移动视角（Moving Views）。提出混合策略（continuous camera motion + diverse static viewpoints），在减少虚假相关性的同时保持训练稳定性。

Result: 混合策略在减少捷径学习方面表现最佳，使VLA模型能够泛化到未见过的相机位姿和物体配置，而单纯增加静态视角则失败。该问题在多种架构中普遍存在，且所有评估模型（ACT、Diffusion、Pi0、Gr00t）均从混合数据策略中显著受益。

Conclusion: 捷径学习的易感性和空间泛化的困难是VLA模型跨架构的普遍特性。混合连续运动与多样静态视角的数据策略是提升空间泛化的有效数据中心解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models have shown remarkable promise in generalized robotic manipulation. However, their spatial generalization remains fragile. We argue that simply increasing the number of viewpoints is insufficient. Models often fall into the trap of Shortcut Learning, latching onto spurious correlations (e.g., fixed relative poses between objects or between the camera and robot base) rather than learning true spatial relationships. In this work, we propose a data-centric solution to enhance VLA spatial generalization. We utilize a dual-arm setup where one arm performs manipulation while the other serves as a mobile environmental camera. We systematically evaluate three data distribution patterns: Fixed, Multi-Fixed, and Moving Views. Our findings reveal that a hybrid strategy, combining continuous camera motion with diverse static viewpoints, yields the best performance by substantially reducing spurious correlations while maintaining training stability. Our experiments demonstrate that this strategy mitigates spurious correlations, enabling VLAs to generalize to unseen camera poses and object configurations where simply adding more static viewpoints fails. Crucially, we reveal that the susceptibility to shortcut learning and the struggle with spatial generalization are universal characteristics shared across diverse architectures. Consequently, all evaluated models (ACT, Diffusion, and VLA models including Pi0 and Gr00t) benefit significantly from our mixed data strategy.

</details>


### [166] [HEFT: Heavy-Payload Full-size Humanoid Teleoperation with Privileged Motion Guidance and Windowed Payload Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02332)
*Chenxin Liu,Qingzhou Lu,Guangxiao Yang,Xuanyang Shi,Chenghan Yang,Yanjiang Guo,Jianyu Chen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: HEFT是一个面向全尺寸重型负载人形机器人的遥操作框架，通过Privileged Motion Guidance（PMG）和Windowed Payload Curriculum（WPC）两大方法，解决了VR追踪噪声/漂移和大惯性带来的鲁棒性问题，并在L7人形机器人（175cm/65kg）上实现了最高24kg负载下的运动跟踪。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有通用运动跟踪与遥操作框架多在小平台或无真实负载交互下验证，全尺寸人形机器人在大惯性和紧平衡裕度下对VR追踪噪声/漂移/重定向误差极为敏感，同时其负载潜力未被充分利用。作者旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 1) Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG)：从可部署的有噪VR参考中学习，通过物理合理的重构参考进行引导；2) Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC)：采用窗口化负载课程与专家引导的负载上限，逐步学习鲁棒的重负载跟踪策略。

Result: 在L7全尺寸人形机器人（175cm/65kg）上部署，实现了转身、前进/后退行走、深蹲等动作的跟踪，最高可承载24kg负载。

Conclusion: HEFT证明了全尺寸人形机器人在真实负载交互下进行鲁棒运动跟踪的可行性，为重型负载遥操作技能获取提供了可扩展的学习框架。

Abstract: General motion tracking and teleoperation offer a promising path to scalable humanoid skill acquisition, yet most existing frameworks are validated on compact platforms or without real payload interaction, leaving full-size humanoids with real payloads largely unexplored. Scaling to full-size humanoids introduces two compounding challenges: their larger inertia and tighter balance margins make tracking highly sensitive to noise, drift, and retargeting errors from commodity VR trackers, while their payload potential remains largely underutilized. We present HEFT, a heavy-payload full-size humanoid teleoperation framework that addresses both challenges. HEFT learns from deployable noisy VR references with physically plausible reconstructed references through Privileged Motion Guidance (PMG), and uses a Windowed Payload Curriculum (WPC) with expert-guided payload caps to acquire robust heavy-payload tracking. We deploy HEFT on L7, a 175cm, 65kg humanoid. The robot tracks motions including turns, forward/backward locomotion, and squats under payloads up to 24kg.

</details>


### [167] [ACID: Action Consistency via Inverse Dynamics for Planning with World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02403)
*Gawon Seo,Dongwon Kim,Suha Kwak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出ACID框架，通过循环动作一致性（逆动力学模型从预测状态反推的动作应与原动作一致）改进决策时规划，在多种世界模型和任务中提升规划质量并降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准规划代价仅检查预测终点与目标的接近程度，未验证中间轨迹的可实现性，导致预测轨迹看似合理但实际环境 rollout 偏离。

Method: 提出ACID框架，引入循环动作一致性约束：利用逆动力学模型从预测的相邻状态反推动作，并与原条件动作计算残差作为规划代价的一部分，通过尺度不变的自适应权重融合。

Result: 在4种动作条件世界模型、6个任务（刚体/可变形操作、关节控制、视觉导航）上，ACID持续提升规划质量，在显著降低计算量的同时达到与基线相当的精度。

Conclusion: 循环动作一致性是提升决策时规划可靠性和效率的有效机制。

Abstract: Decision-time planning with action-conditioned world models has become a popular paradigm for embodied control. However, the standard planning cost judges a candidate solely by how close its predicted terminal state lies to the goal, leaving the realizability of the intermediate transitions unchecked -- a predicted trajectory can look convincing while the environment rollout drifts away from it. In this paper, we propose ACID, a decision-time planning framework that introduces cycle action consistency: the action inferred backward from a predicted transition by an inverse dynamics model should recover the one that was conditioned on. We fold this per-step residual into the planning cost via a scale-invariant adaptive weight. Across four action-conditioned world models and six tasks spanning rigid and deformable manipulation, articulated control, and visual navigation, ACID consistently improves planning and matches the baseline's accuracy with substantially less planning compute.

</details>


### [168] [LIME: Learning Intent-aware Camera Motion from Egocentric Video](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02417)
*Boyang Sun,Jiajie Li,Yung-Hsu Yang,Chenyangguang Zhang,Tim Engelbracht,Sunghwan Hong,Cesar Cadena,Marc Pollefeys,Hermann Blum*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出了LIME模型，将语言指令与相机运动生成相结合，通过从自我中心视频中挖掘多意图监督信号，实现基于自然语言意图的机器人主动相机运动控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主机器人常需移动相机以观察目标、揭示遮挡区域或响应用户意图，但语言条件化的相机运动作为一类动作尚未得到充分探索。

Method: LIME模型结合了自回归的观察增益输出和连续流匹配位姿头，从自我中心视频中挖掘多意图相机运动监督信号，将合理的意图和观察增益描述与相对SE(3)目标位姿配对。

Result: 在实验和下游机器人任务中，LIME能够从被动的人类视频中学习主动选择相机位姿，将普通自我中心录像转化为意图感知主动感知的监督信号。

Conclusion: 语言条件化相机运动生成是可行的，通过从自我中心视频中学习可以有效地将自然语言意图映射到具体的相机运动，实现意图感知的主动感知能力。

Abstract: Autonomous robots often need to move their camera before they can act: to inspect an object, reveal an occluded region, or obtain a view that responds to a user's intent. While vision-language navigation translates instructions to base motion and vision-language-action policies map instructions to manipulation actions, language-conditioned camera motion remains comparatively underexplored as a first-class action. We formulate language-conditioned camera motion generation: given a current RGB observation and a free-form natural-language intent, predict a relative target camera pose for the next observation. This task is inherently non-trivial: viewpoint changes are driven by latent perceptual intentions, and a valid motion may operate at different semantic granularity, from entering a room to looking around a corner, inspecting a visible object, or revealing an occluded detail. To model this structure, we mine multi-intention camera-motion supervision from egocentric video, pairing plausible intents and observation-gain descriptions with relative SE(3) target poses. We propose LIME, a vision-language camera-motion generator that combines an auto-regressive observation-gain output with a continuous flow-matching pose head. This design lets the model jointly predict what the next view should reveal while representing multi-hypothesis target views. Across experiments and downstream robotic tasks, we show that LIME can learn to actively choose camera poses from passive human video, turning ordinary egocentric recordings into supervision for intent-aware active perception.

</details>


### [169] [WorldSample: Closed-loop Real-robot RL with World Modelling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02431)
*Yuquan Xue,Le Xu,Zeyi Liu,Zhenyu Wu,Zhengyi Gu,Xinyang Song,Bofang Jia,Ziwei Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WorldSample提出了一种基于物理真实数据的增强框架，通过真实轨迹与世界模型生成的合成数据形成闭环，结合策略 paced learning 进行样本选择与调度，在降低视觉幻觉的同时提升机器人强化学习效率，在操作任务上实现成功率提升28%、训练步骤减少59%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实机器人强化学习面临交互成本高的问题，每次物理 rollout 代价昂贵且只能反映一条动作-结果路径；模仿学习受限于演示覆盖范围，而直接使用世界模型生成数据存在视觉幻觉问题。

Method: 提出WorldSample框架，包含：(1) 物理真实轨迹与世界模型生成高保真合成数据的闭环；(2) Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) 策略，通过样本选择与调度平衡有用增强与价值高估，减轻幻觉噪声。

Result: 在接触类与精密操作任务上，相比基线政策成功率提升28%，训练步骤减少59%；世界模型视觉保真度提升19.4dB PSNR与0.47 SSIM。

Conclusion: 物理真实与合成数据的闭环能同时提升策略与世界模型性能，PPL有效缓解了合成数据带来的价值高估问题，验证了该方法在真实机器人RL中的有效性。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) can overcome the demonstration-coverage limitation of imitation learning (IL) by allowing robots to improve through trial-and-error interaction beyond the states observed in demonstrations. However, deploying RL on real robots remains constrained by high interaction costs, since each physical rollout is costly and reflects only one realized action-outcome path. To address this challenge, we propose WorldSample, a physically grounded data augmentation framework for real-robot RL that closes a real-synthetic loop between physical rollouts, world-model generation, and policy improvement. Grounded on real rollouts, WorldSample generates high-fidelity synthetic transitions through a post-trained world model, which greatly lowers the visual hallucination. Specifically, rather than simply using these transitions as real-world experience, WorldSample introduces Policy-Paced Learning (PPL) to regulate the training process through sample selection and scheduling, balancing useful augmentation against value overestimation and mitigating the hallucination-induced noise. Experiments on robot manipulation tasks involving contact-rich and precise tasks show that WorldSample improves policy success rate by 28% while reducing training steps by 59% compared with baselines. Furthermore, WorldSample improves world model visual fidelity by 19.4dB in PSNR and 0.47 in SSIM over demonstration-only post-training, validating the effectiveness of the real-synthetic loop for both policy and world model performance.

</details>


### [170] [Learning to Move Before Learning to Do: Task-Agnostic pretraining for VLAs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02466)
*Junhao Shi,Siyin Wang,Xiaopeng Yu,Li Ji,Jingjing Gong,Xipeng Qiu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务无关预训练（TAP）框架，将VLA模型解耦为物理能力获取（自监督逆动力学学习）与语义对齐（少量语言监督）两个阶段，在SIMPLER基准上以数量级更少的标注数据匹配百万级专家轨迹性能，并在真实机器人平台上展现出更强的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA模型受限于专家示范数据稀缺，其根本原因在于将物理能力（如何移动）与语义对齐（做什么）两种学习目标混为一谈，而后者才需要语言监督。

Method: 提出解耦预训练假设，将学习分解为两阶段：第一阶段通过自监督逆动力学目标从廉价、无标签的交互数据（包括离轨轨迹和自主机器人探索）中学习可迁移的运动先验；第二阶段使用极少量专家数据将这些先验与语言对齐。

Result: 在SIMPLER基准上，TAP以数量级更少的标注数据匹配超过100万专家轨迹训练模型的性能，绝对提升10%；在真实WidowX平台上，相机扰动下TAP保持25%成功率，而互联网规模基线降至0%。

Conclusion: 任务无关预训练能产生鲁棒、可迁移的物理表征，为具身AI提供可扩展的发展路径。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) models are fundamentally bottlenecked by the scarcity of expert demonstrations -- triplets of observations, instructions, and actions that are costly to collect at scale. We argue that this bottleneck stems from conflating two distinct learning objectives: acquiring physical competence (how to move) and acquiring semantic alignment (what to do). Crucially, only the latter requires language supervision. Building on this Decomposition Hypothesis, we propose Task-Agnostic Pretraining (TAP), a two-stage framework that first learns transferable motor priors from cheap, unlabeled interaction data -- including discarded off-task trajectories and autonomous robot play -- via a self-supervised Inverse Dynamics objective. A lightweight second stage then grounds these priors in language using minimal expert data. On the SIMPLER benchmark, TAP matches models trained on over 1M expert trajectories while using orders of magnitude less labeled data, yielding a 10% absolute gain over standard behavior cloning. On a real-world WidowX platform, TAP retains 25% success under camera perturbations where internet-scale baselines collapse to 0%, demonstrating that task-agnostic pretraining produces robust, transferable physical representations and offers a scalable path forward for Embodied AI.

</details>


### [171] [Learning Agile Intruder Interception using Differentiable Quadrotor Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02472)
*Michael Anoruo,Xiaoyu Tian,Abhishek Rathod,Timothy Naudet,Thomas Canchola,Eric Sturzinger,Kshitij Goel,Wennie Tabib*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种利用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态的深度强化学习方法，用于学习拦截入侵者的控制策略，在高达10米/秒的速度下实现敏捷拦截，性能比基线方法平均提升30%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有深度强化学习方法假设可以获取相对位置或距离信息，但在实际应用中（如被动单目相机传感器）这些信息难以获得，因此需要一种仅依赖方向信息的高效拦截策略。

Method: 利用可微分的四旋翼动力学模型，结合解析策略梯度方法，仅使用3D方向单位向量和拦截器状态来学习控制策略。

Result: 所提出方法在高达10米/秒的速度下实现敏捷拦截，性能比基于简化质点动力学的基线方法平均提升30%。

Conclusion: 所提出的方法在仅依赖方向信息的条件下，能够有效学习高速拦截策略，显著优于传统简化模型方法。

Abstract: This paper presents a methodology for learning a control policy to intercept an intruder using the 3D direction unit vector to the intruder and the interceptor state. Prior deep reinforcement learning approaches assume either relative position or distance to the intruder is available, but this information is not readily accessible in real-world applications that employ passive, monocular camera sensors. Instead, we propose a solution that leverages an analytical policy gradient method using differentiable quadrotor dynamics to learn agile interception at speeds up to 10 m/s. The proposed approach outperforms baseline methods that utilize simplified point mass dynamics by an average of 30%.

</details>


### [172] [QuadRocket: An Aerial Robotic Testbed for Adaptive Thrust-Vector Control of Rocket-Like Vehicles](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02474)
*Pedro Santos,Joel Reis,Paulo Oliveira,Carlos Silvestre*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了QuadRocket——一种基于四旋翼的火箭原型平台，用于低成本、低风险地验证运载器级别的推力矢量控制策略。通过将四旋翼作为推力矢量执行器，设计了自适应反步控制器实现几乎全局轨迹跟踪，并通过动态面姿态控制器跟踪期望推力矢量。仿真和实验验证了该平台的精确轨迹跟踪与扰动补偿能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推力矢量控制策略的验证通常依赖高成本、高风险的运载器平台，缺乏灵活、低成本的地面测试平台。QuadRocket旨在填补这一空白，提供一个能模拟火箭动力学特性的安全、可重复测试环境。

Method: 将四旋翼与火箭主体通过万向节耦合，建模为单轴对称刚体系统；采用二维球面上的降维姿态表示，利用轴对称性解耦偏航与推力矢量方向；设计自适应反步控制器实现存在未知恒定扰动时的几乎全局轨迹跟踪；通过控制点变换缓解非最小相位行为；将四旋翼视为推力矢量执行器，设计基于动态面的姿态控制器以避免虚拟控制信号的显式微分。

Result: 仿真与室内运动捕捉场地的实验表明：该系统实现了精确的轨迹跟踪和有效的扰动补偿，验证了QuadRocket作为推力矢量控制机器人平台的有效性。

Conclusion: QuadRocket是一种低成本、低风险、适用于验证先进推力矢量控制策略的通用测试平台，其控制架构具有良好的轨迹跟踪性能和鲁棒性。

Abstract: This paper presents QuadRocket, a quadrotor-based rocket prototype that provides a low-cost, low-risk platform for validating advanced thrust-vector control strategies for launch vehicle-type systems. The prototype consists of a cylindrical main body mounted on top of a quadrotor through a universal joint, forming a flying inverted pendulum with non-negligible inertia. For control design, the coupled system is modeled as a single axisymmetric rigid body actuated by a vectored force applied along its longitudinal axis. A reduced-attitude representation on the two sphere is adopted to explicitly exploit the vehicle's axial symmetry and to decouple yaw from the thrust-vector direction. On this model, we derive an adaptive backstepping controller that achieves almost global trajectory tracking in the presence of unknown constant disturbances, while a control-point transformation mitigates non minimum-phase behavior. The quadrotor is then treated as a thrust vector actuator, and a dynamic-surface-based attitude controller is designed to track the desired thrust-vector, accounting for actuation dynamics and avoiding explicit differentiation of virtual control signals. The complete architecture is evaluated in simulation and validated experimentally in an indoor motion-capture arena. Results demonstrate accurate trajectory tracking, effective disturbance compensation, and confirm the suitability of the QuadRocket as a versatile testbed for thrust-vector-controlled robotic vehicles.

</details>


### [173] [Controllable Sim Agents with Behavior Latents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02496)
*Juanwu Lu,Junyu Zhu,Ziran Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出CNeVA，一种可控神经变分智能体框架，通过闭式共轭变分更新从分通道折扣回报中推断行为隐变量，并结合整流流轨迹生成器实现可控仿真。在Waymo数据集上达到竞争性逼真度，同时提供分通道的可控性，包括速度、加速度和安全性控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实交通仿真需要既能模仿日志行为又能沿可解释轴进行控制的智能体，以便工程师隔离变量、复现边缘场景并安全测试自动驾驶系统。

Method: 1) 从分通道折扣回报中通过闭式共轭变分更新推断每个智能体的高斯行为隐变量；2) 训练基于整流流（rectified-flow）的轨迹生成器，采用混合通道掩码课程学习实现无分类器引导；3) 提出软合格门（soft eligibility gates），用平滑指数衰减替代硬二进制阈值以保留近阈值智能体的梯度信号。

Result: 在Waymo Open Motion Dataset上达到竞争性逼真度；速度和加速度控制产生单调响应且无停滞诱导的奖励破解；引入软合格门后安全性控制单调且显著；实现了上下文残差回报度量下的可引导地图合规性。

Conclusion: CNeVA在保持逼真度的同时提供了分通道可控性，这是排名更高的模仿模型所缺乏的。实验表明，引导度量必须与物理合理性防护措施一起解读，以避免奖励破解的混淆效应。

Abstract: Realistic traffic simulation requires agents that imitate logged behavior and can also be steered along interpretable axes. Such controllability enables engineers to isolate variables, reproduce specific edge cases, and test autonomous systems without real-world risk. We introduce Controllable Neural Variational Agents (CNeVA), a controllable simulated-agent framework that learns to infer a per-agent Gaussian behavior latent from per-channel discounted returns via a closed-form conjugate variational update, conditioning a rectified-flow trajectory generator trained on a mixed channel-mask curriculum for classifier-free guidance. To tackle scarcity in reward signals, we propose soft eligibility gates that replace hard binary thresholds with smooth exponential decay, preserving the gradient signal for near-threshold agents. On the Waymo Open Motion Dataset, CNeVA attains competitive realism on the benchmark while exposing per-channel controllability that the higher-ranked imitation models lack. Speed- and acceleration-based steering produces monotone responses without stall-induced reward hacking. Safety controllability is monotone and substantial with the introduction of soft eligibility. We manage to achieve steerable map compliance under a context-residual return measure. Furthermore, our experiment demonstrates that steering metrics must be read alongside physical-plausibility guardrails to avoid reward-hacking confounds.

</details>


### [174] [Embodied.cpp: A Portable Inference Runtime of Embodied AI Models on Heterogeneous Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02501)
*Ling Xu,Chuyu Han,Borui Li,Hao Wu,Shiqi Jiang,Ting Cao,Chuanyou Li,Sheng Zhong,Shuai Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Embodied.cpp，一个面向具身AI模型的便携式C++推理运行时，针对VLA模型和世界-动作模型（WAM）的部署需求，通过五层架构设计实现多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理和可扩展接口，在异构设备上取得高任务成功率和显著的内存优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有推理运行时主要为请求-响应式服务设计，无法满足具身部署的运行时契约：闭环控制中的多速率执行、异构硬件上的延迟优先批量1推理、以及超越固定token I/O的可扩展具身接口。具身AI模型的部署仍碎片化于各种Python栈、后端假设和机器人胶水代码中。

Method: 通过对代表性VLA模型和WAM的架构分析，提取共享执行路径，将其组织为五层架构：输入适配器（Input Adapters）、序列构建器（Sequence Builders）、主干执行（Backbone Execution）、头插件（Head Plugins）和部署适配器（Deployment Adapters）。运行时提供模块化多速率执行、延迟优先融合推理以及可扩展的算子和I/O支持，通过单一后端抽象实现跨异构设备、机器人和模拟器的部署。

Result: 在HY-VLA和pi0.5两个VLA模型上分别实现100.0%和91.0%的闭环任务成功率；在基于LingBot-VA Transformer块的WAM基准测试中，将块内存从312.2 MiB压缩至88.1 MiB。

Conclusion: Embodied.cpp在保持高精度的同时提升了多种具身模型架构的部署效率，验证了其作为统一推理运行时的有效性。

Abstract: Embodied AI models now span vision-language-action (VLA) models and world-action models (WAMs), but practical deployment remains fragmented across model-specific Python stacks, backend assumptions, and robot-side glue code, especially on heterogeneous edge devices. Existing inference runtimes are designed mainly for request-response serving and therefore do not satisfy the runtime contract of embodied deployment: multi-rate execution inside closed-loop control, latency-first batch-1 inference on heterogeneous hardware, and extensible embodied interfaces beyond fixed token I/O. We present Embodied.cpp, a portable C++ inference runtime for embodied models. Based on an architectural analysis of representative VLA models and WAMs, Embodied.cpp captures a shared execution path and organizes it into five layers: input adapters, sequence builders, backbone execution, head plugins, and deployment adapters. The runtime provides modular multi-rate execution, latency-first fused inference, and extensible operator and I/O support, enabling deployment across heterogeneous devices, robots, and simulators through one backend abstraction. We evaluate Embodied.cpp on two VLA models, HY-VLA and pi0.5, and on a preliminary WAM benchmark using a LingBot-VA Transformer block. The VLA deployments achieve successful closed-loop execution with 100.0% and 91.0% task success rates, respectively. The WAM benchmark reduces block memory from 312.2 MiB to 88.1 MiB. These results show that Embodied.cpp improves deployment efficiency while preserving high accuracy across diverse embodied model architectures.

</details>


### [175] [VT-WAM: Visual-Tactile World Action Model for Contact-Rich Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.02503)
*Shuai Tian,Yupeng Zheng,Yuhang Zheng,Songen Gu,Yujie Zang,Yuxing Qin,Weize Li,Haoran Li,Wenchao Ding,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VT-WAM提出了一种视觉-触觉世界动作模型，通过统一流匹配框架联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，采用非对称混合Transformer注意力和接触门控注意力引导，在六项接触丰富操作任务中平均成功率71.67%，显著优于基线方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 接触丰富的操作任务需要策略对局部变形、压力、滑移和摩擦等触觉线索做出反应，但这些线索在时间上稀疏且视觉观测中往往不可见。现有视觉-触觉策略通常直接将触觉观测馈入动作预测，很少在动作生成过程中建模触觉变形动力学。

Method: (1) 非对称混合Transformer注意力：将首帧视觉锚点与时间触觉动力学桥接；(2) 接触门控动作-视觉-触觉注意力引导：在接触阶段鼓励动作查询依赖触觉证据。整体框架基于统一流匹配，联合学习未来视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测。

Result: 在六项真实世界接触丰富操作任务中，VT-WAM平均成功率达71.67%，比Fast-WAM高26.67%，比OmniVTLA高35.84%。消融实验表明建模触觉变形动力学和引导接触阶段触觉注意力对接触丰富任务均至关重要。

Conclusion: VT-WAM通过统一流匹配框架联合建模视觉预测、触觉变形预测和动作预测，有效提升了接触丰富操作任务的表现，验证了触觉变形动力学建模和接触阶段注意力引导的重要性。

Abstract: Contact-rich manipulation requires policies to react to local deformation, pressure, slip, and friction, yet these cues are temporally sparse and often invisible in visual observations. Existing visual-tactile policies usually feed tactile observations directly into action prediction, but rarely model tactile deformation dynamics during action generation. In this paper, we introduce VT-WAM, a Visual-Tactile World Action Model that jointly learns future visual prediction, tactile deformation prediction, and action prediction within a unified flow matching framework. In particular, VT-WAM introduces (1) Asymmetric Mixture-of-Transformers (MoT) attention to bridge a first-frame visual anchor with temporal tactile dynamics, and (2) contact-gated Action-Visual-Tactile Attention Guidance (AVTAG) to encourage action queries to rely on tactile evidence during contact phases. Across six real-world contact-rich manipulation tasks, VT-WAM achieves a 71.67% average success rate, outperforming Fast-WAM by 26.67% and OmniVTLA by 35.84%. Ablations demonstrate that modeling tactile deformation dynamics and guiding contact-phase tactile attention are both important for contact-rich tasks. Project website: https://vt-wam.github.io/.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [176] [PACE: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Plausible and Actionable Counterfactual Explanations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01306)
*Pavel Iakovets,Liyanapathiranage Sudeepika Wajirakumari Samarathunga,Martin Thomas Horsch,Fadi Al Machot*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PACE是一个模块化神经符号框架，将神经网络预测与符号推理（ASP规则）结合，生成符合领域约束的可行性感知反事实解释，在Adult Income数据集上验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反事实解释方法缺乏显式机制来融入领域知识和干预约束，导致生成的解释往往不现实或不可行。

Method: 提出PACE框架，分离预测与推理：神经网络模型负责分类，符号推理层（使用Answer Set Programming编码领域约束）在反事实生成过程中强制执行可行干预，生成符合领域知识的可解释、可操作的解释。

Result: 在Adult Income数据集上的案例研究表明，符号约束生成的反事实解释能更好地满足领域特定的可行性要求，揭示了反事实有效性与合理性之间的权衡。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法为透明、可行性感知的反事实解释提供了一条有前景的路径，PACE框架模型无关且可适应需要现实决策支持的领域。

Abstract: Counterfactual explanations explain machine learning predictions by identifying minimal input changes that would alter a model's decision. Although many existing methods successfully generate prediction-changing alternatives, they often produce unrealistic or infeasible recommendations due to a lack of explicit mechanisms for incorporating domain knowledge and intervention constraints. Neuro-symbolic AI offers a promising direction by combining data-driven predictive models with symbolic reasoning capable of representing human-understandable rules and feasible actions. This paper presents PACE, a modular neuro-symbolic framework for generating feasibility-aware counterfactual explanations. The framework separates prediction and reasoning into two components: a neural predictive model for classification and a symbolic reasoning layer that enforces domain-specific constraints during counterfactual generation. By explicitly modeling feasible interventions, the framework produces explanations consistent with domain knowledge while remaining interpretable and actionable. The approach is model-agnostic and adaptable to domains requiring realistic decision support. A case study is conducted on the Adult Income dataset, combining a multilayer perceptron classifier with Answer Set Programming (ASP) rules encoding feasible modifications to education, occupation, and working hours while preserving immutable attributes. Results highlight the trade-off between counterfactual validity and plausibility and show that symbolic constraints yield explanations that better satisfy domain-specific feasibility requirements, illustrating the potential of neuro-symbolic methods for transparent, feasibility-aware counterfactual explanation in explainable AI.

</details>


### [177] [Auto-FL-Research: Agentic Search for Federated Learning Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01366)
*Holger R. Roth,Ziyue Xu,Chester Chen,Daguang Xu,Peter Cnudde,Andrew Feng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Auto-FL-Research (AFR)，一种基于编码智能体的约束工作流，用于自动化搜索联邦学习算法配方（包括优化器、聚合规则、本地训练策略等）。在多个医疗和LEAF数据集上评估，发现部分提升来自真正的FL机制改进，部分来自固定表面调参或单次运行的随机伪影。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦学习研究中的许多算法选择（优化器、聚合规则、训练调度等）琐碎但影响重大，手工探索成本高昂且难以公平比较，尤其是在候选变更可能同时改变训练或评估路径的情况下。

Method: 提出AFR平台，采用编码智能体工作流：智能体可提出并实现候选训练算法（聚合规则、客户端更新调度、局部目标、模型变体），任务配置文件固定变异表面、计算预算、通信契约和最终评估。每次实验记录候选分数、运行时间、编辑文件、产物及失败状态。

Result: 在5个FLamby医疗任务和5个固定LEAF数据集+合成任务上评估，五种子重复实验支持FLamby的4个任务和LEAF的5/6个profile有提升，同时暴露了种子敏感和搜索选中的失败案例。相同预算控制显示：部分增益对应真正的FL配方变更，另一些可被固定表面标量控制恢复，或在重复/保留评估下失效。

Conclusion: 混合结果表明智能体生成的候选可区分为三类：可重复的FL机制改进、固定表面调参效应、以及单次运行的随机伪影。这为自动化FL算法搜索提供了系统性分析方法。

Abstract: Federated learning (FL) research often depends on many small but consequential algorithmic choices: optimizer variants, server aggregation rules, local training schedules, normalization, regularization, and model architecture. These choices are expensive to explore manually and difficult to compare fairly when candidate changes can also alter the FL training or evaluation path. In this work, we present Auto-FL-Research (AFR), a constrained coding-agent workflow for FL algorithmic recipe search. Agents may propose and implement candidate training algorithms, including server aggregation rules, client update schedules, local objectives, and registered model variants, while task profiles fix the mutation surface, compute budget, communication contract, and final model evaluation. Each campaign records candidate scores, runtime, edited files, artifacts, and failure status. We evaluate AFR on five healthcare cross-silo FLamby tasks and on grouped-client profiles for the five fixed LEAF datasets plus the LEAF synthetic task. Five-seed repeat evaluations support gains on four FLamby tasks and five of six LEAF profiles, while also exposing seed-sensitive and search-selected failure cases. Same-budget controls show that several gains correspond to FL-recipe changes, whereas other improvements are recovered by fixed-surface scalar controls or fail under repeat or held-out evaluation. These mixed outcomes are part of the contribution: they show how agent-generated candidates can be separated into repeated FL mechanisms, fixed-surface tuning effects, and selected single-run artifacts.

</details>


### [178] [The Wiola Architecture for Efficient Small Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01394)
*Aryuemaan Kumar Chowdhury,Afreen Shaik,Yaparla Bhargavi,Brahma Kumar*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Wiola，一个完全原创的小型语言模型架构，包含五种独立创新组件：螺旋旋转位置编码（SRPE）、门控跨层注意力（GCLA）、自适应token合并（ATM）、双流前馈网络（DSFF）和WiolaRMSNorm。提供完整数学推导和与GPT-2等模型的对比，发布四个规格版本（120M-1.5B参数），兼容HuggingFace生态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM架构（GPT、LLaMA等）高度同质化，缺乏从根本上重新设计的探索。作者旨在从第一性原理出发构建全新SLM架构，引入多种创新组件以提升效率和性能。

Method: 提出五种创新组件：1) SRPE：在三维螺旋流形上嵌入位置编码，融合绝对、相对和层次位置信号；2) GCLA：每层可跨层软注意力访问前两层的压缩摘要；3) ATM：中间层动态合并语义冗余的相邻token以降低注意力复杂度；4) DSFF：双并行流经学习到的逐维度门控融合替代传统MLP；5) WiolaRMSNorm：引入逐维度学习偏移向量防止表示坍缩。

Result: 发布四个模型规格（120M、360M、700M、1.5B参数），所有22个架构单元测试通过，完全兼容HuggingFace Transformers生态系统。

Conclusion: Wiola展示了一种完全原创的SLM架构设计路径，通过多种创新组件协同工作，为小语言模型提供了新的设计范式参考。

Abstract: We present Wiola, a fully original Small Language Model (SLM) architecture built from first principles, sharing no structural lineage with any existing model family including GPT, LLaMA, Mistral, or Falcon. Wiola introduces five independently novel components: (i) Spiral Rotary Positional Encoding (SRPE), which embeds token positions on a three-dimensional helical manifold combining absolute, relative, and hierarchical positional signals; (ii) Gated Cross-Layer Attention (GCLA), providing each decoder layer with soft cross-attention access to compressed summaries of two preceding layers for inter-layer coherence; (iii) Adaptive Token Merging (ATM), which dynamically merges se mantically redundant adjacent tokens in middle network layers to reduce attention complexity without information loss; (iv) Dual Stream Feed-Forward (DSFF), replacing the conventional MLP with two parallel streams fused by a learned per-dimension gate; and (v) WiolaRMSNorm, a modified normalisation introducing a per-dimension learned offset vector that prevents representation collapse. We provide complete mathematical derivations, architectural block diagrams, complexity analyses, and systematic comparisons against GPT-2, LLaMA-2, and Mistral. Wiola is released in four sizes (120M, 360M, 700M, and 1.5B parameters) and is fully compatible with the HuggingFace Transformers ecosystem, with all 22 architectural unit tests passing.

</details>


### [179] [Agent4cs: A Multi-agent System for Code Summarization in Large Hierarchical Codebases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01425)
*Yongjian Tang,Ezgi Sarikayak,Doruk Tuncel,Jie M. Zhang,Thomas Runkler*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多智能体框架Agent4cs，通过自底向上的方式对大型代码库进行摘要生成，包含摘要生成、关键词提取和质量保证三个智能体，在7个前沿模型上平均语义一致性提升8%，关键词覆盖率提升最多38%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有代码摘要方法将源代码视为纯文本，未充分利用仓库中的层级依赖关系；且多数方案依赖单一语言模型，难以处理结构混乱、文档不全的大型代码库。

Method: 提出Agent4cs多智能体框架，采用自底向上策略：(1) 摘要生成智能体负责生成鲁棒的摘要；(2) 关键词提取智能体主动识别子文件夹中的关键信息；(3) 质量保证智能体迭代优化输出的可读性、连贯性和完整性。

Result: 在7个前沿模型上，相比两个结构化提示基线，所有文件夹级别的语义一致性平均提升8%；在真实数据集上，归一化关键词覆盖率最高提升38%。

Conclusion: Agent4cs通过多智能体协作和自底向上策略，有效利用代码仓库的层次结构信息，显著优于传统基于单一模型或纯文本方法的摘要生成效果。

Abstract: Understanding large, complex codebases, especially those with obfuscated structures and incomplete documentation, remains a significant challenge. Existing code summarization solutions often rely on a single language model or coding assistant like Claude Code, and treat source code as flat text, underutilizing the rich interdependencies and hierarchical information within a repository. To address these shortcomings, we propose Agent4cs - a multi-agent framework that summarizes large codebases in a bottom-up fashion, where a summarization agent focuses on producing robust summaries; a keyword-extraction agent proactively identifies critical information from subfolders; and a quality-assurance agent iteratively refines the outputs for readability, coherence, and completeness. Evaluated on 7 frontier models, Agent4cs improves semantic consistency across all folder levels by average 8% compared to two structured prompting baselines with code segments. Furthermore, extensive evaluation on real-world datasets demonstrates up to 38% gains in normalized keyword coverage rate over the same baselines.

</details>


### [180] [World Feedback for Clinical Agents: Diagnosing RL in FHIR Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01470)
*Ananya Mantravadi,Harshit Rajgarhia,Prasanna Desikan,Abhishek Mukherji*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)，审计发现先前基准存在41.7%的静默完成上限，新基准降至8.9%。训练Qwen3-8B发现两大结构性障碍：能力上限（10/20任务类型零基础性能）和格式-知识障碍（3/20类型需要不可探索的精确临床编码）。纯RL仅达18.2% pass@1，规则SFT达34.1%，差距完全由上述障碍导致。提出决策/格式-知识/查找分类法预测RL可学习性，并给出解决方案：SFT注入编码知识，RL学习条件逻辑。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床协议执行任务（如检查化验值、应用阈值、生成结构化FHIR医嘱）是RL从世界反馈中学习的天然候选场景——临床专家将决策逻辑编码为验证器后，该验证器可对无限次尝试进行评分，无需逐条标注。但应用RL需要健全的反馈渠道和足够的基础能力。

Method: 审计MedAgentBench v1/v2发现41.7%的静默完成上限；构建MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)（508个任务，8.9%上限）；训练Qwen3-8B识别两大障碍；提出决策/格式-知识/查找分类法。

Result: 纯RL达18.2% pass@1，规则SFT达34.1%，15.9个百分点的差距完全由能力上限和格式-知识障碍导致。

Conclusion: 决策/格式-知识/查找分类法可预测RL可学习性；SFT注入编码知识，RL学习条件逻辑是修复方案。

Abstract: Clinical protocol-execution tasks -- checking a lab value, applying a threshold, placing a correctly structured FHIR order -- are natural candidates for RL from world feedback: once clinical SMEs encode decision logic into a verifier, that verifier grades unlimited rollouts without per-episode annotation. But applying RL requires a sound feedback channel and sufficient base capability. We audit MedAgentBench v1/v2, find a 41.7\% silent-finish ceiling that makes inaction the RL dominant strategy, and construct \textbf{MedAgentBench-v3 (MAB-v3)} (508 tasks, 8.9\% ceiling). Training Qwen3-8B exposes two structural barriers: a \emph{capability ceiling} (10/20 task types have 0\% base performance, zero gradient) and a \emph{format-knowledge barrier} (3/20 types require exact clinical codes undiscoverable by exploration). Pure RL reaches 18.2\% pass@1 vs.\ 34.1\% for rule-based SFT; the 15.9~pp gap is attributable entirely to these barriers. A decision/format-knowledge/lookup taxonomy predicts RL learnability and prescribes the fix: SFT to inject codes, RL to learn conditionals.

</details>


### [181] [Procedural Memory Distillation: Online Reflection for Self-Improving Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01480)
*Ye Liu,Srijan Bansal,Bo Pang,Yang Li,Zeyu Leo Liu,Yifei Ming,Zixuan Ke,Shafiq Joty,Semih Yavuz*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出**过程记忆蒸馏（PMD）**，一种在强化学习与可验证奖励（RLVR）框架下，将跨回合/跨周期的过程性信号（轨迹、策略、行为模式）转化为可复用记忆并蒸馏进策略权重的方法。在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。核心设计是**共同进化**：策略生成回合更新记忆，记忆塑造监督更新策略，冻结任一方均导致超10%的性能下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR及自蒸馏变体（如SDPO）仅利用回合级别的验证信号，忽略了回合/周期内丰富的过程性信息（如哪些策略持续通过验证、哪些失败模式反复出现、哪些行为模式重复发生）。这些跨回合信号无法被局部更新捕获，造成信息浪费。

Method: PMD构建三层抽象过程记忆：原始轨迹、自反思策略与经验、跨问题重复的高层行为模式。记忆在线从模型自身轨迹中提取。一个**记忆条件化的自教师**利用累积经验对学生模型自身rollout进行监督，使学生逐步将过程性知识内化到参数中。核心是**共同进化**循环：策略生成轨迹→更新记忆→记忆塑造监督→更新策略。

Result: 在Qwen3-8B和OLMo3-Instruct-7B上，PMD相比SDPO在SCIKNOWEVAL提升3.8-5.5%，在LIVECODEBENCH提升7.9-13.6%。消融实验表明，冻结记忆或策略均导致在SCIKNOWEVAL各领域上性能下降超过10%。

Conclusion: 过程记忆蒸馏通过跨回合信号重用和共同进化机制，显著提升了RLVR训练效率，且推理时无需额外记忆开销。记忆与策略的共同进化是性能提升的关键。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR), along with recent selfdistillation variants such as SDPO, evaluates each rollout against a verifier and updates the policy from that episode-level signal. However, the richer procedural information in the rollout is rarely retained or reused. Across episodes and epochs, the model repeatedly encounters related problems under a changing policy, producing cross-episode signals that episode-local updates cannot capture: which strategies consistently pass verification, which failure modes persist, which patterns recur. We propose Procedural Memory Distillation (PMD), which converts these crossepisode signals into reusable procedural memory and distills it into the policy's weights during training. This memory functions as a training scaffold, absorbed into the policy itself, yielding a memory-free model at inference. PMD organizes the memory at three levels of abstraction: raw trajectories, self-reflected strategies and lessons, and higher-level behavioral patterns that recur across problems, all extracted online from the model's own trajectories. A memory-conditioned self-teacher draws on the accumulated experience to supervise the student on its own rollouts, enabling student to progressively internalize procedural knowledge within its parameters. The central design principle is co-evolution: the policy generates rollouts that update the memory, and memory shapes the supervision that updates the policy. Empirically, across Qwen3-8B and OLMo3-Instruct-7B, PMD improves over SDPO by 3.8-5.5% on SCIKNOWEVAL and 7.9-13.6% on LIVECODEBENCH. Co-evolution powers these gains: freezing either the memory or the policy trails PMD by more than 10% across SCIKNOWEVAL domains.

</details>


### [182] [Autonomous discovery of traffic laws with AI traffic scientists](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.01639)
*Xingyuan Dai,Yue Liu,Xiaoyan Gong,Qinghai Miao,Junyou Shang,Yutong Wang,Chao Guo,Yonglin Tian,Yizhang Chai,Chao Xiang,Yisheng Lv,Fei-Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了TrafficSci，一个自主AI系统，能够通过迭代工作流（证据筛选、批判性假设归纳、观察与干预验证）自动发现城市交通规律，在四个案例中重新发现了三项已知交通定律，并识别出一个新的城市驾驶行为内在时间记忆尺度，该尺度在八个城市和两个轨迹数据集中具有统计一致性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 交通定律的发现目前依赖专家驱动，需要从异构观测证据中识别候选规律或通过干预实验验证，缺乏自动化、可扩展的方法。将AI科学发现从受控实验室环境扩展到复杂的交通领域仍是一大挑战。

Method: TrafficSci是一个智能体AI系统，将交通定律发现形式化为可迭代、可审计的工作流，包含三个核心模块：证据筛选（evidence scoping）、基于批判性评判的假设归纳（critic-judge hypothesis induction）、以及观察与干预验证（observational-interventional validation）。

Result: 在人口、网络、控制和轨迹四个尺度的案例研究中，TrafficSci自主重新发现了三项已有交通定律，并识别出一个未报道的城市驾驶行为内在时间记忆尺度，该尺度在八个城市和两个轨迹数据集中统计一致。

Conclusion: TrafficSci提供了一条从受控领域向复杂城市系统扩展AI驱动科学发现的可行路径。
